*GPT message: The current menu contains options for filtering and sorting cards, which are not related to display scaling or font size. The bug report mentions scaling the "card browser," which is a display preference. Such settings are typically found in a main "Settings" or "Preferences" menu, not in a context menu for data manipulation.

Therefore, the most logical step is to exit this menu and navigate to the main settings area of the app. The 'back' action will close the current popup menu, allowing us to backtrack to the main navigation.

Suggestion: [{'action': 'back'}]
